你是一位经验丰富的dba，你将对当前负载进行的全局参数进行调整，使得它的tps/p95更高。针对于当前服务器，你现在在一个12 vCPU，64GB内存和116GB SSD的硬盘上进行postgres 16.13 (Ubuntu 16.13-0ubuntu0.24.04.1)的sysbench oltp_read_only参数调参。当前负载预识别为sysbench_pg_oltp_read_only，主要调优方向是：postgres_memory_cache_residency, planner_cost_and_io_concurrency, parallelism_and_worker_budget, client_connection_budget, jit_and_tracking_overhead；低优先级方向是：wal_and_checkpoint_write_path, autovacuum_write_cost_policy。这是完整压测脚本以及当前的初始参数，初始的压测是6319.02/13.70 (tps/p95=461.2423)。你需要先基于完整的候选全局参数空间给服务器给出一套全局更好的配置方案，不是只孤立地挑少量 knobs；同时你可以通过观察当前服务器的state_metric进行调整，在少数尝试之后调节到最优tps/lat。第一轮的candidate_config也必须是一套可执行的全局候选配置，而不是只调一两个参数。它应该覆盖 workload_interpretation 指出的主要方向；如果是纯读负载，不要为了凑全局而强行把 redo/binlog/flush 当作核心收益来源。如果是纯benchmark极限目标，可以直接采用更激进的benchmark_max全局组合并说明crash safety风险。请把这套全局方案写入global_config_plan，把同一套第一轮要落地验证的全局参数变更写入candidate_config。

下面是这次输入给你的完整参考文件，文件内容需要像用户直接上传给你一样使用。你必须先阅读压测脚本文件，再结合当前参数、state_metric、历史调优结果判断下一轮要调哪些全局参数。

### benchmark_config: <PAPER_WORKSPACE>\agenticDB\config\benchmark_postgres_10_20_173_60_read.yaml

workload:
  kind: shell

  db_driver: pgsql

  host: "127.0.0.1"
  port: 5432
  output_path: "/tmp/sb_pg.log"

objective:
  primary_metric: "tps"
  direction: "maximize"
  formula: "tps_over_p95"
  latency_metric: "p95_latency_ms"
  min_improvement_ratio: 0.01

  max_metrics: 80
  include_parameter_metadata: true

### benchmark_script: <PAPER_WORKSPACE>\scripts\run_sysbench_pg.sh
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# 说明：
# - PostgreSQL 专用 sysbench 运行脚本（与 scripts/run_sysbench.sh 的接口保持一致）
# - 用法：
#     bash scripts/run_sysbench_pg.sh read 127.0.0.1 5432 <REDACTED_PASSWORD> 60 /tmp/sb_pg.log
# - 可选环境变量（不传则用默认）：
#     PGUSER=postgres
#     PGDB=sbtest
#     SYSBENCH_THREADS=64
#     SYSBENCH_TIME=60
#     SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL=5
#     SYSBENCH_RATE=0   # 0/空表示不限速
#     SYSBENCH_TABLES=32
#     SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=1000000
#
# 前置依赖（Linux/WSL）：
# - sysbench（带 pgsql driver）
# - psql（postgresql-client）
# - /usr/share/sysbench/ 下存在 oltp_read_only.lua 等脚本

script_path="/usr/share/sysbench/"

MODE="${1:-read}"
HOST="${2:-127.0.0.1}"
PORT="${3:-5432}"
PASS="${4:-}"
DURATION="${5:-60}"
OUTFILE="${6:-/tmp/sb_pg.log}"

if [ "${MODE}" == "read" ]; then
  run_script="${script_path}oltp_read_only.lua"
elif [ "${MODE}" == "write" ]; then
  run_script="${script_path}oltp_write_only.lua"
else
  run_script="${script_path}oltp_read_write.lua"
fi

PGUSER="${PGUSER:-postgres}"
PGDB="${PGDB:-sbtest}"

DEFAULT_THREADS=$( [ "${MODE}" = "read" ] && echo 64 || echo 32 )
THREADS="${SYSBENCH_THREADS:-${DEFAULT_THREADS}}"
# allow overriding duration via env (useful for web UI / experiments)
DURATION="${SYSBENCH_TIME:-${DURATION}}"
REPORT_INTERVAL="${SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL:-5}"
TABLES="${SYSBENCH_TABLES:-32}"
TABLE_SIZE="${SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE:-1000000}"

RATE_OPT=""
if [ -n "${SYSBENCH_RATE:-}" ] && [ "${SYSBENCH_RATE}" != "0" ]; then
  RATE_OPT="--rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE}"
fi

if [ ! -f "${run_script}" ]; then
  echo "[ERR] sysbench lua 脚本不存在：${run_script}" >&2
  exit 2
fi

export PGPASSWORD="${PASS}"

# ensure log dir exists and show where to look
mkdir -p "$(dirname "${OUTFILE}")" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
touch "${OUTFILE}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
echo "[INFO] sysbench(pg) log => ${OUTFILE}"

# 0) preflight: connectivity check (gives clearer error than repeated failures later)
if ! psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -tAc "SELECT 1;" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERR] 无法连接 PostgreSQL：host=${HOST} port=${PORT} user=${PGUSER}" >&2
  echo "      常见原因：密码不对 / pg_hba.conf 不允许 password 认证 / 用户名不对。" >&2
  echo "      你可以二选一修复：" >&2
  echo "      A) 直接给 postgres 设置密码（示例）：sudo -u postgres psql -c \"ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';\"" >&2
  echo "      B) 用已有账号跑：export PGUSER=<你的用户名>，并把第 4 个参数改成该用户密码，或配置 ~/.pgpass" >&2
  echo "      另外也可检查：sudo cat /etc/postgresql/*/main/pg_hba.conf（确保 local/host 规则允许 scram/md5）" >&2
  exit 3
fi

# nice to print pg version
echo "[INFO] Connected. Server version:"
psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -tAc "SHOW server_version;" | sed 's/^/[INFO]   /' || true

# 1) ensure db exists
echo "[INFO] Ensure database exists: ${PGDB}"
if ! psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -tAc "SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname='${PGDB}'" | grep -q 1; then
  psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -q -c "CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};" || true
fi

# 2) ensure tables prepared once
echo "[INFO] Check prepared tables in db=${PGDB} (expect ${TABLES} tables, size=${TABLE_SIZE})"
tbl_cnt=$(psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d "${PGDB}" -tAc "SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name LIKE 'sbtest%';" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
if [ -z "${tbl_cnt}" ]; then tbl_cnt=0; fi
if [ "${tbl_cnt}" -ne "${TABLES}" ]; then
  echo "[INFO] Need prepare: found ${tbl_cnt} tables, expected ${TABLES}. Will recreate DB and run sysbench prepare (this may take a long time)."
  # drop and recreate to avoid partial prepare conflicts
  psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -q -c "SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname='${PGDB}';" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -q -c "DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS ${PGDB};" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  psql -h "${HOST}" -p "${PORT}" -U "${PGUSER}" -d postgres -q -c "CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

  echo "[INFO] Preparing data via sysbench prepare..."
  echo "[INFO]   tip: you can speed up first run by setting SYSBENCH_TABLES=8 SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=100000"
  {
    echo "$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --tables=${TABLES} --table-size=${TABLE_SIZE} prepare"
    sysbench "${run_script}" \
    --pgsql-host="${HOST}" \
    --pgsql-port="${PORT}" \
    --pgsql-user="${PGUSER}" \
    --pgsql-password="${PASS}" \
    --pgsql-db="${PGDB}" \
    --db-driver=pgsql \
    --tables="${TABLES}" \
    --table-size="${TABLE_SIZE}" \
    prepare
  } >> "${OUTFILE}" 2>&1 || true
else
  echo "[INFO] Prepared tables already present: ${tbl_cnt}/${TABLES}."
fi

# 3) run
echo "[INFO] Running benchmark: mode=${MODE} threads=${THREADS} time_s=${DURATION} report_interval=${REPORT_INTERVAL} rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE:-0}"
{
  echo "$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --threads=${THREADS} --time=${DURATION} run"
  sysbench "${run_script}" \
  --pgsql-host="${HOST}" \
  --pgsql-port="${PORT}" \
  --pgsql-user="${PGUSER}" \
  --pgsql-password="${PASS}" \
  --pgsql-db="${PGDB}" \
  --db-driver=pgsql \
  --range-size=100 \
  --events=0 \
  --rand-type=uniform \
  --tables="${TABLES}" \
  --table-size="${TABLE_SIZE}" \
  --report-interval="${REPORT_INTERVAL}" \
  --threads="${THREADS}" \
  --time="${DURATION}" \
  ${RATE_OPT} \
  run
} >> "${OUTFILE}" 2>&1 || true

echo "[INFO] Done. Tail of log:"
tail -n 30 "${OUTFILE}" | sed 's/^/[LOG] /' || true


```

### reference_file: <PAPER_WORKSPACE>\readme_pg.md
```text
### L2TTune：PostgreSQL + sysbench（一阶段热启动/LHS）全流程

本文档把 `readme.md`（MySQL 版本）的关键流程，整理成 **PostgreSQL 版本**，用于：

- **PostgreSQL（端口 5432）**
- **sysbench（pgsql driver）**
- 目标：先跑通压测，再做 **一阶段热启动（LHS）**，产出 `knob_metric.txt/state_metric.txt`，为后续 RF/TD3 做数据准备。

---

### 0. 初始化

**获取初始化值**

```
python3 tuner/tools/dump_pg_vars_from_csv.py \
  --input pg_knobs_tunable.csv \
  --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5432 --user postgres --password <REDACTED_PASSWORD> \
  --database postgres \
  --output pg_knobs_initial.csv
```

***恢复初始化***

```


CSV_PATH=pg_knobs_initial.csv \
HOST=127.0.0.1 PORT=5432 USER=postgres PASS=<REDACTED_PASSWORD> \
RESET_ALL=1 \
bash scripts/restore_pg_from_csv.sh

# 如果 pg_knobs_tunable.csv 里包含 postmaster 参数，恢复后补一次重启
sudo systemctl restart postgresql
```

**能不能调**

```
psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U postgres -d postgres -c \
"SELECT name, context, vartype, unit FROM pg_settings WHERE name IN ('shared_memory_size','huge_page_size','huge_pages','shared_memory_type');"
```

### 清除日志

```
chmod +x scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh

sudo bash -c "nohup bash scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh \
  --path / \
  --threshold_gb 20 \
  --interval_s 600 \
  --pg_log_dir /var/log/postgresql \
  --journal_vacuum_size 200M \
  --project_dir <LOCAL_USER_HOME>/L2TTune \
  --clear_caches \
  >>/var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log 2>&1 &"

sudo tail -f /var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log

#删掉脚本
sudo fuser -v /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock
sudo pkill -9 -f "auto_clean_pg_logs.sh" || true
sleep 1
sudo bash -lc 'flock -n /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock -c "echo FREE" || echo LOCKED'
sudo rm -f /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock
sudo bash -c "nohup bash scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh --path / --threshold_gb 20 --interval_s 600 --pg_log_dir /var/log/postgresql --journal_vacuum_size 200M --clear_caches >>/var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log 2>&1 &"
sudo tail -f /var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log
```

### 重建，大清除空间

```
# 1) 停服务
sudo systemctl stop postgresql@16-main || sudo systemctl stop postgresql || true

# 2) 删除整个 cluster（会清空 /var/lib/postgresql/16/main）
sudo pg_dropcluster --stop 16 main

# 3) 重建并启动
sudo pg_createcluster 16 main --start

# 4) 设密码（按你项目默认）
sudo -u postgres psql -c "ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';"

# 5) 验证 + 看空间
PGPASSWORD=<REDACTED_PASSWORD> psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U postgres -d postgres -c "select 1;"
df -hT /
```

DISK=$(df -B1 /var/lib/postgresql/16/main | awk 'NR==2{print $2}')
CORES=$(nproc --all)

  /dev/null \

  --hint_order=2 --use_implicit=1 --filter_params=1 \

### knob_space_file: <PAPER_WORKSPACE>\pg_knobs_common150_ranges.csv
```csv

### state_metric_whitelist: <PAPER_WORKSPACE>\environment\metrics_whitelist pg.py

请先使用后面 JSON 里的 `workload_interpretation` 作为负载预识别结果，再交叉检查压测脚本和实际执行命令。不要因为 wrapper 脚本中包含未执行的 readwrite/write 分支，就把 `mode: read` 的负载误判成 readwrite。调参方向必须优先跟随 `workload_interpretation.primary_tuning_directions` 和 `workload_interpretation.knobs_to_prioritize`；`workload_interpretation.knobs_to_deprioritize` 只在 state_metric 明确反证时才作为主要方向。

当前初始参数在后面 JSON 的 `current_config` 和 `allowed_knob_space` 中。`allowed_knob_space` 是候选全局参数空间，当前值已经由接口重新获取；不要修改不在这个空间里的数据库参数。

如果 `allowed_knob_space` 中包含来自多个候选来源的补充项，你可以一起评估这些候选，但不要猜测或修改未出现在 `allowed_knob_space` 里的参数。跨 DBMS 的隔离、参数名合法性和范围检查由 AgenticDB 的配置、候选空间和 validator 负责。

第一轮你必须先基于 `allowed_knob_space` 形成一套全局更好配置方案，而不是只孤立挑少量 knobs。请在 `global_config_plan` 中说明你从全局候选空间中如何分组判断、哪些准备修改、哪些保持不变以及原因；同时第一轮的 `candidate_config` 也必须是一套可执行的全局候选配置，而不是一两个局部 probe。对于 DB 第一轮，它应该覆盖 `workload_interpretation` 指出的主要子系统；read-only 负载不要强行将 redo/binlog/durability 作为主要收益方向，readwrite/write 负载才需要重点考虑 redo、binlog、flush 和写路径。如果第一轮少于 8 个 DB knobs，必须在 `diagnosis` 里明确解释为什么不能做全局多 knob 尝试。后续轮次才可以为了拆分定位或细调，使用较小的增量变更。

如果当前目标是纯 benchmark 极限值，并且 `allowed_knob_space` 中存在需要重启或有风险的候选参数，可以基于 workload、state_metric、当前配置和历史结果选择更激进的 `benchmark_max` 路线。静态候选要设置 `restart_required=true`，并在 `risk` 里明确说明可能牺牲 crash safety、恢复能力、观测能力或生产安全性。不要自己写 shell 命令，直接把要验证的全局/启动参数写入 `candidate_config`，AgenticDB 会负责持久化配置、重启、warmup 和压测验证。

后续轮次不要重复第一轮完整说明，而是基于已有上下文、历史成功/失败结果和最新 state_metric 继续尝试更高的 tps/p95。之前所有会话和本次已完成轮次的调优结果在后面 JSON 的 `tuning_history_summary` 中。里面包括每次尝试配置了什么、成功还是失败、得到的 tps/p95/score 和回滚/接受原因。你必须继承这些结果，成功的方向可以继续细调，失败的组合不要重复；如果失败只是因为“在线 SET 不允许，需要重启”，则可以改成静态重启路线重新验证。

当前服务器的 state_metric 在后面 JSON 的 `db_metrics` 和 `os_metrics` 中。你需要结合 state_metric、当前参数、初始压测和最近几轮历史，在少数尝试之后调节到更优的 tps/lat。

当前阶段是 `db`。如果当前阶段是 `db`，只返回数据库全局参数配置；如果当前阶段是 `os_sysctl`，只返回 `allowed_os_knob_space` 里的内核 sysctl 参数，`action_type` 写 `os_config`；如果当前阶段是 `os_control`，只返回 `allowed_os_control_space` 里的系统控制项，`action_type` 写 `os_control`。阶段切换由 auditor 判断。

只返回一个 JSON 对象，格式如下：

-c \"SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname='${PGDB}';\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"DROP DATABASE           "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# 说明：\n# - PostgreSQL 专用 sysbench 运行脚本（与 scripts/run_sysbench.sh 的接口保持一致）\n# - 用法：\n#     bash scripts/run_sysbench_pg.sh read 127.0.0.1 5432 <REDACTED_PASSWORD> 60 /tmp/sb_pg.log\n# - 可选环境变量（不传则用默认）：\n#     PGUSER=postgres\n#     PGDB=sbtest\n#     SYSBENCH_THREADS=64\n#     SYSBENCH_TIME=60\n#     SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL=5\n#     SYSBENCH_RATE=0   # 0/空表示不限速\n#     SYSBENCH_TABLES=32\n#     SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=1000000\n#\n# 前置依赖（Linux/WSL）：\n# - sysbench（带 pgsql driver）\n# - psql（postgresql-client）\n# - /usr/share/sysbench/ 下存在 oltp_read_only.lua 等脚本\n\nscript_path=\"/usr/share/sysbench/\"\n\nMODE=\"${1:-read}\"\nHOST=\"${2:-127.0.0.1}\"\nPORT=\"${3:-5432}\"\nPASS=\"${4:-}\"\nDURATION=\"${5:-60}\"\nOUTFILE=\"${6:-/tmp/sb_pg.log}\"\n\nif [ \"${MODE}\" == \"read\" ]; then\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_read_only.lua\"\nelif [ \"${MODE}\" == \"write\" ]; then\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_write_only.lua\"\nelse\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_read_write.lua\"\nfi\n\nPGUSER=\"${PGUSER:-postgres}\"\nPGDB=\"${PGDB:-sbtest}\"\n\nDEFAULT_THREADS=$( [ \"${MODE}\" = \"read\" ] && echo 64 || echo 32 )\nTHREADS=\"${SYSBENCH_THREADS:-${DEFAULT_THREADS}}\"\n# allow overriding duration via env (useful for web UI / experiments)\nDURATION=\"${SYSBENCH_TIME:-${DURATION}}\"\nREPORT_INTERVAL=\"${SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL:-5}\"\nTABLES=\"${SYSBENCH_TABLES:-32}\"\nTABLE_SIZE=\"${SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE:-1000000}\"\n\nRATE_OPT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"${SYSBENCH_RATE:-}\" ] && [ \"${SYSBENCH_RATE}\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  RATE_OPT=\"--rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE}\"\nfi\n\nif [ ! -f \"${run_script}\" ]; then\n  echo \"[ERR] sysbench lua 脚本不存在：${run_script}\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nexport PGPASSWORD=\"${PASS}\"\n\n# ensure log dir exists and show where to look\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"${OUTFILE}\")\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\ntouch \"${OUTFILE}\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\necho \"[INFO] sysbench(pg) log => ${OUTFILE}\"\n\n# 0) preflight: connectivity check (gives clearer error than repeated failures later)\nif ! psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SELECT 1;\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"[ERR] 无法连接 PostgreSQL：host=${HOST} port=${PORT} user=${PGUSER}\" >&2\n  echo \"      常见原因：密码不对 / pg_hba.conf 不允许 password 认证 / 用户名不对。\" >&2\n  echo \"      你可以二选一修复：\" >&2\n  echo \"      A) 直接给 postgres 设置密码（示例）：sudo -u postgres psql -c \\\"ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';\\\"\" >&2\n  echo \"      B) 用已有账号跑：export PGUSER=<你的用户名>，并把第 4 个参数改成该用户密码，或配置 ~/.pgpass\" >&2\n  echo \"      另外也可检查：sudo cat /etc/postgresql/*/main/pg_hba.conf（确保 local/host 规则允许 scram/md5）\" >&2\n  exit 3\nfi\n\n# nice to print pg version\necho \"[INFO] Connected. Server version:\"\npsql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SHOW server_version;\" | sed 's/^/[INFO]   /' || true\n\n# 1) ensure db exists\necho \"[INFO] Ensure database exists: ${PGDB}\"\nif ! psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname='${PGDB}'\" | grep -q 1; then\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -q -c \"CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};\" || true\nfi\n\n# 2) ensure tables prepared once\necho \"[INFO] Check prepared tables in db=${PGDB} (expect ${TABLES} tables, size=${TABLE_SIZE})\"\ntbl_cnt=$(psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d \"${PGDB}\" -tAc \"SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name LIKE 'sbtest%';\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nif [ -z \"${tbl_cnt}\" ]; then tbl_cnt=0; fi\nif [ \"${tbl_cnt}\" -ne \"${TABLES}\" ]; then\n  echo \"[INFO] Need prepare: found ${tbl_cnt} tables, expected ${TABLES}. Will recreate DB and run sysbench prepare (this may take a long time).\"\n  # drop and recreate to avoid partial prepare conflicts\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname='${PGDB}';\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS ${PGDB};\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\n  echo \"[INFO] Preparing data via sysbench prepare...\"\n  echo \"[INFO]   tip: you can speed up first run by setting SYSBENCH_TABLES=8 SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=100000\"\n  {\n    echo \"$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --tables=${TABLES} --table-size=${TABLE_SIZE} prepare\"\n    sysbench \"${run_script}\" \\\n    --pgsql-host=\"${HOST}\" \\\n    --pgsql-port=\"${PORT}\" \\\n    --pgsql-user=\"${PGUSER}\" \\\n report_interval=${REPORT_INTERVAL} rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE:-0}\"\n{\n  echo \"$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --threads=${THREADS} --time=${DURATION} run\"\n  sysbench \"${run_script}\" \\\n           "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# 说明：\n# - PostgreSQL 专用 sysbench 运行脚本（与 scripts/run_sysbench.sh 的接口保持一致）\n# - 用法：\n#     bash scripts/run_sysbench_pg.sh read 127.0.0.1 5432 <REDACTED_PASSWORD> 60 /tmp/sb_pg.log\n# - 可选环境变量（不传则用默认）：\n#     PGUSER=postgres\n#     PGDB=sbtest\n#     SYSBENCH_THREADS=64\n#     SYSBENCH_TIME=60\n#     SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL=5\n#     SYSBENCH_RATE=0   # 0/空表示不限速\n#     SYSBENCH_TABLES=32\n#     SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=1000000\n#\n# 前置依赖（Linux/WSL）：\n# - sysbench（带 pgsql driver）\n# - psql（postgresql-client）\n# - /usr/share/sysbench/ 下存在 oltp_read_only.lua 等脚本\n\nscript_path=\"/usr/share/sysbench/\"\n\nMODE=\"${1:-read}\"\nHOST=\"${2:-127.0.0.1}\"\nPORT=\"${3:-5432}\"\nPASS=\"${4:-}\"\nDURATION=\"${5:-60}\"\nOUTFILE=\"${6:-/tmp/sb_pg.log}\"\n\nif [ \"${MODE}\" == \"read\" ]; then\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_read_only.lua\"\nelif [ \"${MODE}\" == \"write\" ]; then\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_write_only.lua\"\nelse\n  run_script=\"${script_path}oltp_read_write.lua\"\nfi\n\nPGUSER=\"${PGUSER:-postgres}\"\nPGDB=\"${PGDB:-sbtest}\"\n\nDEFAULT_THREADS=$( [ \"${MODE}\" = \"read\" ] && echo 64 || echo 32 )\nTHREADS=\"${SYSBENCH_THREADS:-${DEFAULT_THREADS}}\"\n# allow overriding duration via env (useful for web UI / experiments)\nDURATION=\"${SYSBENCH_TIME:-${DURATION}}\"\nREPORT_INTERVAL=\"${SYSBENCH_REPORT_INTERVAL:-5}\"\nTABLES=\"${SYSBENCH_TABLES:-32}\"\nTABLE_SIZE=\"${SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE:-1000000}\"\n\nRATE_OPT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"${SYSBENCH_RATE:-}\" ] && [ \"${SYSBENCH_RATE}\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  RATE_OPT=\"--rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE}\"\nfi\n\nif [ ! -f \"${run_script}\" ]; then\n  echo \"[ERR] sysbench lua 脚本不存在：${run_script}\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nexport PGPASSWORD=\"${PASS}\"\n\n# ensure log dir exists and show where to look\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"${OUTFILE}\")\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\ntouch \"${OUTFILE}\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\necho \"[INFO] sysbench(pg) log => ${OUTFILE}\"\n\n# 0) preflight: connectivity check (gives clearer error than repeated failures later)\nif ! psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SELECT 1;\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"[ERR] 无法连接 PostgreSQL：host=${HOST} port=${PORT} user=${PGUSER}\" >&2\n  echo \"      常见原因：密码不对 / pg_hba.conf 不允许 password 认证 / 用户名不对。\" >&2\n  echo \"      你可以二选一修复：\" >&2\n  echo \"      A) 直接给 postgres 设置密码（示例）：sudo -u postgres psql -c \\\"ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';\\\"\" >&2\n  echo \"      B) 用已有账号跑：export PGUSER=<你的用户名>，并把第 4 个参数改成该用户密码，或配置 ~/.pgpass\" >&2\n  echo \"      另外也可检查：sudo cat /etc/postgresql/*/main/pg_hba.conf（确保 local/host 规则允许 scram/md5）\" >&2\n  exit 3\nfi\n\n# nice to print pg version\necho \"[INFO] Connected. Server version:\"\npsql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SHOW server_version;\" | sed 's/^/[INFO]   /' || true\n\n# 1) ensure db exists\necho \"[INFO] Ensure database exists: ${PGDB}\"\nif ! psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -tAc \"SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname='${PGDB}'\" | grep -q 1; then\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -q -c \"CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};\" || true\nfi\n\n# 2) ensure tables prepared once\necho \"[INFO] Check prepared tables in db=${PGDB} (expect ${TABLES} tables, size=${TABLE_SIZE})\"\ntbl_cnt=$(psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d \"${PGDB}\" -tAc \"SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='public' AND table_name LIKE 'sbtest%';\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\nif [ -z \"${tbl_cnt}\" ]; then tbl_cnt=0; fi\nif [ \"${tbl_cnt}\" -ne \"${TABLES}\" ]; then\n  echo \"[INFO] Need prepare: found ${tbl_cnt} tables, expected ${TABLES}. Will recreate DB and run sysbench prepare (this may take a long time).\"\n  # drop and recreate to avoid partial prepare conflicts\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname='${PGDB}';\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS ${PGDB};\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  psql -h \"${HOST}\" -p \"${PORT}\" -U \"${PGUSER}\" -d postgres -q -c \"CREATE DATABASE ${PGDB};\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\n  echo \"[INFO] Preparing data via sysbench prepare...\"\n  echo \"[INFO]   tip: you can speed up first run by setting SYSBENCH_TABLES=8 SYSBENCH_TABLE_SIZE=100000\"\n  {\n    echo \"$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --tables=${TABLES} --table-size=${TABLE_SIZE} prepare\"\n    sysbench \"${run_script}\" \\\n    --pgsql-host=\"${HOST}\" \\\n    --pgsql-port=\"${PORT}\" \\\n    --pgsql-user=\"${PGUSER}\" \\\n    --pgsql-password=\"${PASS}\" \\\n    --pgsql-db=\"${PGDB}\" \\\n    --db-driver=pgsql \\\n    --tables=\"${TABLES}\" \\\n    --table-size=\"${TABLE_SIZE}\" \\\n    prepare\n  } >> \"${OUTFILE}\" 2>&1 || true\nelse\n  echo \"[INFO] Prepared tables already present: ${tbl_cnt}/${TABLES}.\"\nfi\n\n# 3) run\necho \"[INFO] Running benchmark: mode=${MODE} threads=${THREADS} time_s=${DURATION} report_interval=${REPORT_INTERVAL} rate=${SYSBENCH_RATE:-0}\"\n{\n  echo \"$ sysbench ${run_script} --db-driver=pgsql --threads=${THREADS} --time=${DURATION} run\"\n  sysbench \"${run_script}\" \\\n  --pgsql-host=\"${HOST}\" \\\n  --pgsql-port=\"${PORT}\" \\\n  --pgsql-user=\"${PGUSER}\" \\\n  --pgsql-password=\"${PASS}\" \\\n  --pgsql-db=\"${PGDB}\" \\\n  --db-driver=pgsql \\\n  --range-size=100 \\\n  --events=0 \\\n  --rand-type=uniform \\\n  --tables=\"${TABLES}\" \\\n  --table-size=\"${TABLE_SIZE}\" \\\n  --report-interval=\"${REPORT_INTERVAL}\" \\\n  --threads=\"${THREADS}\" \\\n  --time=\"${DURATION}\" \\\n  ${RATE_OPT} \\\n  run\n} >> \"${OUTFILE}\" 2>&1 || true\n\necho \"[INFO] Done. Tail of log:\"\ntail -n 30 \"${OUTFILE}\" | sed 's/^/[LOG] /' || true\n\n"

目标：先跑通压测，再做 **一阶段热启动（LHS）**，产出 `knob_metric.txt/state_metric.txt`，为后续 RF/TD3 做数据准备。\n\n---\n\n### 0. 初始化\n\n**获取初始化值**\n\n```\npython3 tuner/tools/dump_pg_vars_from_csv.py \\\n  --input pg_knobs_tunable.csv \\\n  --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5432 --user postgres --password <REDACTED_PASSWORD> \\\n  --database postgres \\\n  --output pg_knobs_initial.csv\n```\n\n***恢复初始化***\n\n```\n\n\nCSV_PATH=pg_knobs_initial.csv \\\nHOST=127.0.0.1 PORT=5432 USER=postgres PASS=<REDACTED_PASSWORD> \\\nRESET_ALL=1 \\\nbash scripts/restore_pg_from_csv.sh\n\n# 如果 pg_knobs_tunable.csv 里包含 postmaster 参数，恢复后补一次重启\nsudo systemctl restart postgresql\n```\n\n**能不能调**\n\n```\npsql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U postgres -d postgres -c \\\n\"SELECT name, context, vartype, unit FROM pg_settings WHERE name IN ('shared_memory_size','huge_page_size','huge_pages','shared_memory_type');\"\n```\n\n### 清除日志\n\n```\nchmod +x scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh\n\nsudo bash -c \"nohup bash scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh \\\n  --path / \\\n  --threshold_gb 20 \\\n  --interval_s 600 \\\n  --pg_log_dir /var/log/postgresql \\\n  --journal_vacuum_size 200M \\\n  --project_dir <LOCAL_USER_HOME>/L2TTune \\\n  --clear_caches \\\n  >>/var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log 2>&1 &\"\n\nsudo tail -f /var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log\n\n#删掉脚本\nsudo fuser -v /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock\nsudo pkill -9 -f \"auto_clean_pg_logs.sh\" || true\nsleep 1\nsudo bash -lc 'flock -n /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock -c \"echo FREE\" || echo LOCKED'\nsudo rm -f /var/lock/l2ttune_log_clean.lock\nsudo bash -c \"nohup bash scripts/auto_clean_pg_logs.sh --path / --threshold_gb 20 --interval_s 600 --pg_log_dir /var/log/postgresql --journal_vacuum_size 200M --clear_caches >>/var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log 2>&1 &\"\nsudo tail -f /var/log/l2ttune_binlog_clean.log\n```\n\n### 重建，大清除空间\n\n```\n# 1) 停服务\nsudo systemctl stop postgresql@16-main || sudo systemctl stop postgresql || true\n\n# 2) 删除整个 cluster（会清空 /var/lib/postgresql/16/main）\nsudo pg_dropcluster --stop 16 main\n\n# 3) 重建并启动\nsudo pg_createcluster 16 main --start\n\n# 4) 设密码（按你项目默认）\nsudo -u postgres psql -c \"ALTER USER postgres PASSWORD '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';\"\n\n# 5) 验证 + 看空间\nPGPASSWORD=<REDACTED_PASSWORD> psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U postgres -d postgres -c \"select 1;\"\ndf -hT /\n```\n\n### 1. 一键安装 PostgreSQL + sysbench(pgsql) 并自检\n\n在项目根目录执行（推荐）：\n\n```bash\nbash '<REDACTED_PASSWORD>';\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. “建库+灌数（prepare）”脚本（与 MySQL 口径对齐）\n\n```bash\ncd scripts\nchmod +x prepare_pg.sh\n./prepare_pg.sh read 127.0.0.1 5432 <REDACTED_PASSWORD> 0 /tmp/sb_pg_prepare.log\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. 压测脚本\n\n```bash\nbash scripts/run_sysbench_pg.sh read 127.0.0.1 5432 <REDACTED_PASSWORD> 60 /tmp/sb_pg.log\nbash scripts/run_sysbench_pg.sh write 127.0.0.1
